**Facts the doc misses**

- ENABLE_SERVICE_WORKER no longer exists. Service workers (46 files under workers/service plus SWServer) are always compiled, and the only switch is the runtime preference ServiceWorkersEnabled (UnifiedWebPreferences.yaml:5644; WebCore default false).
- USE_SYSTEM_MALLOC is a hard #error on Windows (bmalloc/bmalloc/BPlatform.h:431-433). Use USE_MIMALLOC=ON there; it is vendored at Source/bmalloc/mimalloc, MI_OVERRIDE is forced OFF, and it needs CMake >= 3.25.
- USE_LCMS (default ON, WebKitFeatures.cmake:360) is missing from the doc's flag table. Without it, embedded ICC profiles are ignored: WebCore decoders do color management only through LCMS, and ImageBackingStoreSkia.cpp:39 always uses sRGB.
- USE_SKIA defaults OFF (WebKitFeatures.cmake:363), so the port must set it ON. USE_SKIA_ENCODERS (default ON, depends on USE_SKIA) is required for toDataURL and toBlob (skia/ImageUtilitiesSkia.cpp:91-101).
- The vendored Skia CMake hard-requires Fontconfig and FreeType on every non-Windows target (ThirdParty/skia/CMakeLists.txt:4-6, 845-852). WebCore Skia font code calls SkFontMgr_New_FontConfig and FcFontSort directly outside Windows and Android (FontCacheSkia.cpp:70; SkiaSystemFallbackFontCache.cpp:29-110). Removing Fontconfig means new code, not a dependency swap.
- The vendored Skia CMake builds only Ganesh GL/GLES (SK_GL, SK_GANESH, SK_ASSUME_GL_ES=1 at :1070-1086). It has no Metal, Vulkan, D3D or Graphite, although those sources are in the tree. It does build the PDF backend unconditionally (src/pdf from :611).
- WebCore's CMake always links some GL stack: ANGLE EGL/GLES, or libepoxy, or OpenGL::GLES + EGL_LIBRARIES (WebCore/CMakeLists.txt:2299-2316). On Linux that means a link-time dependency on libEGL and libGLESv2, not dlopen. On macOS there is no system EGL, so ANGLE (which has Metal.cmake) is effectively mandatory.
- HarfBuzz must be built with its ICU bridge (libharfbuzz-icu). WebCore links HarfBuzz::ICU (platform/Skia.cmake) and calls hb_icu_script_to_script (ComplexTextControllerSkia.cpp:265; LocaleICU.cpp:82).
- libwebp is not optional. WEBPImageDecoder is compiled unconditionally and WebP::demux is always linked (platform/ImageDecoders.cmake). Skia encoders need WebP::mux, and static builds need libsharpyuv (OptionsWin.cmake:168, PlatformWin.cmake:247-248).
- WebCore uses zlib directly, beyond HTTP and PNG: CompressionStream (Modules/compression/ZStream.h), WebSocket permessage-deflate (WebSocketDeflater.cpp), and WOFF1 (WOFFFileFormat.cpp). ANGLE links zlib as well.
- FreeType built with brotli (FT_CONFIG_OPTION_USE_BROTLI) sets HAVE_WOFF_SUPPORT and makes libwoff2 unnecessary wherever Skia rasterizes through FreeType (WOFF2Checks.cmake; OptionsGTK.cmake:413-421; FontCustomPlatformDataSkia.cpp:139). Windows Skia uses DirectWrite, so libwoff2 is still needed there, linked together with Brotli::dec and WOFF2::common (PlatformWin.cmake:236-244).
- On Windows, the upstream Skia build uses DirectWrite (skia/CMakeLists.txt:815-827, SK_TYPEFACE_FACTORY_DIRECTWRITE), not FreeType. Using FreeType on Windows for embedded fonts means changing Skia's CMake and WebCore's FontCacheSkiaWin.cpp.
- No upstream CMake port builds WebCore with ENABLE_VIDEO=OFF. PlayStation keeps VIDEO ON with no media engine, Windows uses Media Foundation. VIDEO=OFF is untested and likely has bit-rot, while 'VIDEO ON with no engine' is a tested configuration.
- Web Audio needs platform code that exists only for Cocoa and GStreamer: AudioDestination, FFTFrame (there is an FFTFrameStub) and AudioFileReader. A media player backend alone is not enough for the 'standard' profile.
- Media engines are registered in hard-coded code (MediaPlayer.cpp:326-367), so a custom FFmpeg engine means patching buildMediaEnginesVector.
- The curl network glue (NetworkDataTaskCurl, NetworkSessionCurl, NetworkStorageSessionCurl) lives in Source/WebKit/NetworkProcess/curl, not WebCore. WebCore has no ResourceHandleCurl.cpp, so the single-process port must provide its own loader on top of WebCore's CurlRequest.
- curl must use an OpenSSL-API TLS backend: WebCore installs a CURLOPT_SSL_CTX_FUNCTION and works with SSL_CTX* (CurlSSLVerifier.cpp:41-49). It also RELEASE_ASSERTs CURL_VERSION_LARGEFILE (CurlContext.cpp:129) and automatically uses HTTP/3 if curl supports it (CurlContext.cpp:133-134).
- MIME type lookup needs a platform implementation. GLib ports use the vendored ThirdParty/xdgmime (USE_XDGMIME), Win uses MIMETypeRegistryWin.cpp, PlayStation uses MIMETypeRegistryPlayStation.cpp (a static table, the likely model for ZeroG).
- JSC still reserves large virtual ranges even with system malloc: a 1 GB executable pool on x86_64 and 128 or 512 MB on arm64 (jit/ExecutableAllocator.cpp:94-104), plus an aligned structure heap (heap/StructureAlignedMemoryAllocator.cpp:99-112, tunable through Options::structureHeapSizeInKB).
- Upstream Find modules (FindWebP, FindWOFF2, FindBrotli, FindLibPSL) create UNKNOWN IMPORTED targets with no static transitive dependencies. In a fully static build you must add brotlicommon, sharpyuv, zlib (for png and freetype) and similar libs by hand, as PlatformWin.cmake already does.
- Minimum versions enforced by the upstream Options files: ICU 70.1, CURL 7.87.0, HarfBuzz 1.4.2 (Win) / 2.7.4 (WPE), JPEG 1.5.2, LibXml2 2.9.7 (Win) / 2.9.13 (GLib ports), PNG 1.6.34, SQLite3 3.23.1, ZLIB 1.2.11, LibPSL 0.20.2, Freetype 2.9.0, WOFF2 1.0.2, Fontconfig 2.13.0.
- Required build-time tools: Perl 5.10 with English, FindBin and JSON::PP; Python; Ruby; gperf 3.0.1 (only with WebCore) (WebKitCommon.cmake:277-290, 370). unifdef is vendored (Source/CMakeLists.txt:19-22). Source/WebGPU is always added, for its headers only (Source/CMakeLists.txt:27-28).
- External, not verifiable in this checkout: libpsl's official build is meson/autotools and generating its builtin list needs Python; ICU has no CMake build and data filtering needs ICU's Python buildtool; libjpeg-turbo SIMD needs NASM. These conflict with criterion 2 ('si compila statica con CMake o zig') and need custom build scripts.

<details><summary>Concrete starting material</summary>

```text
Suggested OptionsZeroG.cmake core (between WEBKIT_OPTION_BEGIN/END), derived from WebKitFeatures.cmake:207-397:

WEBKIT_OPTION_BEGIN()
# graphics
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(USE_SKIA PRIVATE ON)
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(USE_SKIA_ENCODERS PRIVATE ON)   # toDataURL; needs WebP::mux + sharpyuv
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(USE_LCMS PRIVATE OFF)           # missing from doc table
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(USE_AVIF PRIVATE OFF)
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(USE_JPEGXL PRIVATE OFF)
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(USE_WOFF2 PRIVATE ON)
# features
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(ENABLE_WEBGL PRIVATE OFF)
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(ENABLE_WEBASSEMBLY PRIVATE OFF)
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(ENABLE_XSLT PRIVATE OFF)
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(ENABLE_WEB_AUDIO PRIVATE OFF)
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(ENABLE_VIDEO PRIVATE OFF)        # untested upstream; fallback: ON with no engine (PlayStation model)
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(ENABLE_REMOTE_INSPECTOR PRIVATE OFF)  # dev: ON + SET_AND_EXPOSE_TO_BUILD(USE_INSPECTOR_SOCKET_SERVER ON)
# already OFF by default, listed for clarity: ENABLE_WEB_RTC, ENABLE_MEDIA_STREAM, ENABLE_ENCRYPTED_MEDIA, ENABLE_GAMEPAD, ENABLE_SPEECH_SYNTHESIS, ENABLE_SPELLCHECK
# allocator
if (WIN32)
  WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(USE_MIMALLOC PRIVATE ON)      # USE_SYSTEM_MALLOC is #error on Windows (BPlatform.h:431)
  WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(USE_SYSTEM_MALLOC PRIVATE OFF)
else ()
  WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(USE_SYSTEM_MALLOC PRIVATE ON)  # or USE_MIMALLOC everywhere (+ -DMI_SECURE=ON)
endif ()
WEBKIT_OPTION_END()
# generic run loop on all OSes (copy of OptionsJSCOnly.cmake:107-109)
SET_AND_EXPOSE_TO_BUILD(USE_GENERIC_EVENT_LOOP 1)
SET_AND_EXPOSE_TO_BUILD(WTF_DEFAULT_EVENT_LOOP 0)
SET_AND_EXPOSE_TO_BUILD(USE_CURL ON)
SET_AND_EXPOSE_TO_BUILD(USE_OPENSSL ON)
SET_AND_EXPOSE_TO_BUILD(USE_HARFBUZZ ON)
# ENABLE_SERVICE_WORKER: does not exist; disable via Settings::serviceWorkersEnabled (WebCore default false)

find_package calls the port must satisfy (WebCore/CMakeLists.txt:2263-2316, platform/Curl.cmake, platform/Skia.cmake, platform/ImageDecoders.cmake, ThirdParty/skia/CMakeLists.txt):
 ICU (data,i18n,uc) >=70.1 | HarfBuzz COMPONENTS ICU | Freetype >=2.9 (non-Win) | Fontconfig >=2.13 (non-Win, unless Skia CMake + FontCacheSkia patched) | CURL >=7.87 (OpenSSL backend, HTTP2, brotli, LARGEFILE, alt-svc) | OpenSSL(BoringSSL) | LibPSL >=0.20.2 (builtin list) | ZLIB >=1.2.11 | Brotli COMPONENTS dec | WOFF2 COMPONENTS dec (+common) | PNG >=1.6.34 | JPEG >=1.5.2 | WebP COMPONENTS demux mux (+ libsharpyuv) | LibXml2 >=2.9.7 | SQLite3 >=3.23.1 (target SQLite3::SQLite3) | GL: ANGLE::EGL/ANGLE::GLES (Win, macOS) or OpenGL::GLES + EGL (Linux).
In-tree (no sysroot build): Skia (Source/ThirdParty/skia, STATIC), ANGLE (Source/ThirdParty/ANGLE, STATIC by default), mimalloc (Source/bmalloc/mimalloc), unifdef, optionally xdgmime.
Windows system libs actually linked: WebCore crypt32 iphlpapi usp10 ws2_32 (PlatformWin.cmake:154-159); WTF DbgHelp shlwapi synchronization winmm (WTF/wtf/PlatformWin.cmake:39); ANGLE dxgi dxguid d3d9 synchronization + runtime LoadLibrary d3dcompiler_47.dll.
Patches you will need: (1) your own or patched ThirdParty/skia/CMakeLists.txt to drop Fontconfig and PDF and add a custom font manager and Metal/Vulkan sources if wanted; (2) FontCacheSkia.cpp / SkiaSystemFallbackFontCache.cpp for non-fontconfig fallback; (3) MIMETypeRegistry implementation (copy MIMETypeRegistryPlayStation.cpp pattern); (4) a loader replacing WebKit/NetworkProcess/curl glue; (5) later, MediaPlayer.cpp buildMediaEnginesVector for an FFmpeg engine.
Key files to read: Source/cmake/WebKitFeatures.cmake, Source/cmake/OptionsWin.cmake (closest reference: Skia+curl+OpenSSL+ANGLE), Source/cmake/OptionsPlayStation.cmake (curl+Skia, no media engine, XSLT/WebGL/WebAudio OFF), Source/cmake/OptionsJSCOnly.cmake, Source/WebCore/PlatformWin.cmake, Source/WebCore/platform/{Curl,OpenSSL,Skia,ImageDecoders,FreeType}.cmake, Source/bmalloc/bmalloc/BPlatform.h:380-477.
```

</details>

FixedVMPoolExecutableAllocator calls PageReservation::tryReserve* (ExecutableAllocator.cpp:375-389), and a failure leaves the reservation empty. VM.cpp:217-222 enableAssembler(): if !ExecutableAllocator::singleton().isValid(), it CRASHes only when Options::crashIfCantAllocateJITMemory, otherwise returns false. InitializeThreading.cpp:103-106: if !canUseJIT then Options::useJIT()=false and notifyOptionsChanged(), which cascades to disableAllJITOptions. Nothing in WebKit queries ProcessDynamicCodePolicy (grep for ProhibitDynamicCode/ProcessDynamicCodePolicy finds nothing outside ThirdParty). Caveat: pages are committed lazily, RWX, via OSAllocator::commit, which calls CRASH… | Whether ACG makes even MEM_RESERVE with PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE fail can only be confirmed by running a test with SetProcessMitigationPolicy(ProhibitDynamicCode). If the host or a DLL turns on ACG after JSC init, the next JIT page commit hard-crashes. ZeroG should query GetProcessMitigationPolicy(ProcessDynamicCodePolicy) itself in 'auto' mode, set useJIT=false explicitly, and document that ACG must not be enabled after zg_runtime_create. |

**Facts the doc misses**

- Windows static link: JS_EXPORT becomes __declspec(dllimport) for any consumer unless JS_NO_EXPORT or STATICALLY_LINKED_WITH_JavaScriptCore is defined (JavaScriptCore/API/JSBase.h:81-94). Inside the library it is dllexport, so a static exe would export all JSC C API symbols. Define JS_NO_EXPORT (and the equivalent for WTF_EXPORT_PRIVATE / JS_EXPORT_PRIVATE / WEBCORE_EXPORT) in the ZeroG port. cmake/WebKitMacros.cmake:914 shows the STATICALLY_LINKED_WITH_<target> convention.
- New port on macOS: WTF/wtf/PlatformLegacy.h:49-77 sets PLATFORM(MAC), and therefore PLATFORM(COCOA), for any OS(MACOS) build unless BUILDING_GTK__/HAIKU/WPE/JSCONLY__ is defined. On Windows PLATFORM(WIN) is set automatically. ZeroG must add its own BUILDING_ZEROG__ branch (a small WTF patch) or Cocoa code paths get pulled in. OptionsJSCOnly.cmake:11 shows the pattern (add_definitions(-DBUILDING_JSCONLY__)).
- JIT address-space reservation: 1 GB of virtual memory on x86_64 (ExecutableAllocator.cpp:101-102 fixedExecutableMemoryPoolSize = 1*GB), reserved at JSC init. Options::jitMemoryReservationSize (used at ExecutableAllocator.cpp:357-358) can shrink it for embedders. Worth exposing next to the heap limits.
- JSC reads JSC_<option> environment variables in release builds on every non-Cocoa platform (Options.cpp:395-412 getenv in overrideOptionWithHeuristic; :1118-1129), plus 'JavaScriptCoreUseJIT' (VM.cpp:213-215). End users can change engine behavior (e.g. JSC_useJIT, dump options). The customization callback runs after env parsing (Options.cpp:1140-1141), so ZeroG must explicitly override any security-relevant option it cares about there.
- Order of initialization: JSC::initialize is std::call_once (InitializeThreading.cpp:88-90), and WebCore calls plain JSC::initialize() from ScriptController::initializeMainThread (ScriptController.cpp:120-126), also reached from commonVMSlow (CommonVM.cpp:54-62). zg_runtime_create must call JSC::initialize(customizationLambda) before any WebCore object exists, or the JIT/Wasm/heap options are silently ignored.
- Options freeze: the first VM::VM() calls Config::finalize() (VM.cpp:568), which makes g_jscConfig/WTF config pages read-only (WTFConfig.cpp:190-211). Any later Options::... write crashes.
- No signal-based VM traps on Windows: ENABLE_SIGNAL_BASED_VM_TRAPS requires HAVE(MACHINE_CONTEXT) (PlatformEnable.h:969-971; PlatformHave.h:235-236 has no OS(WINDOWS)). Windows uses polling traps, which matters for watchdog/termination latency.
- 'Una sola VM JavaScript' holds only for main-thread pages (WebCore/bindings/js/CommonVM.cpp:54-90 commonVMSlow creates one HeapType::Large VM). Workers and worklets get their own VMs (workers/WorkerOrWorkletScriptController.cpp:81-95, 'm_vm != &commonVM()'), so memory limits and stats must sum several VMs.
- The JSC C API callback runs under JSLock::DropAllLocks (API/APICallbackFunction.h:67-70), so every JSValue*/JSObject* call inside a native binding re-takes the API lock. For low-latency sync bindings, keep the number of C API calls per invocation small, or use internal JSC APIs in the generated dispatcher.
- The doc's 'PageConsoleClient' / 'InspectorController' / 'InspectorClient' names are outdated at this revision: FrameConsoleClient (WebCore/page/FrameConsoleClient.cpp), PageInspectorController and InspectorBackendClient (WebCore/inspector/).
- Console delivery is suppressed for ephemeral sessions (FrameConsoleClient.cpp:152, :238), and worker console output never reaches ChromeClient (WorkerGlobalScope.cpp:500-538). Both matter for the 'console in terminal in dev mode' feature.
- Remote-inspector default: WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFINE(ENABLE_REMOTE_INSPECTOR ... ON) in cmake/WebKitFeatures.cmake:309, so the port must explicitly set it OFF for the minimal/standard profiles. On Win, PlatformWin.cmake:10-15 drops inspector/JSGlobalObjectInspectorController.cpp when it is off; check that the ZeroG Platform cmake does the same or still compiles.

<details><summary>Concrete starting material</summary>

```text
Runtime JIT configuration (before any WebCore use):
```cpp
#include <JavaScriptCore/InitializeThreading.h>
#include <JavaScriptCore/Options.h>
#include <JavaScriptCore/ExecutableAllocator.h>
// zg_runtime_create():
#if OS(WINDOWS)
PROCESS_MITIGATION_DYNAMIC_CODE_POLICY p{};
bool acg = GetProcessMitigationPolicy(GetCurrentProcess(), ProcessDynamicCodePolicy, &p, sizeof p) && p.ProhibitDynamicCode;
#endif
JSC::initialize([&] {
    if (cfg.jit == ZG_JIT_OFF || acg) {
        JSC::Options::useJIT() = false;            // cascades to disableAllJITOptions (Options.cpp:731-757,881)
    } else if (cfg.jit == ZG_JIT_ON) {
        JSC::Options::crashIfCantAllocateJITMemory() = true; // or check canUseJIT after init and return error
    }
    if (cfg.disable_wasm) JSC::Options::useWasm() = false;
    if (cfg.jit_reserve_bytes) JSC::Options::jitMemoryReservationSize() = cfg.jit_reserve_bytes; // default 1GB x86_64
});
// after: g_jscConfig.vm.canUseJIT / JSC::Options::useJIT() tells whether JIT is actually active
```
Alternative for 'off': call JSC::ExecutableAllocator::disableJIT() before JSC::initialize (WebKit/Shared/win/AuxiliaryProcessMainWin.cpp:52-53).

Getting the page context (C++ embedding layer, needs private headers):
```cpp
#include <JavaScriptCore/APICast.h>          // private header: toGlobalRef
#include <WebCore/ScriptController.h>
void ZGFrameLoaderClient::dispatchDidClearWindowObjectInWorld(WebCore::DOMWrapperWorld& world) {
    if (&world != &WebCore::mainThreadNormalWorldSingleton()) return;
    auto& frame = /* LocalFrame */;
    if (!originAllowed(frame.document()->securityOrigin())) return;
    JSGlobalContextRef ctx = toGlobalRef(frame.script().globalObject(world));
    installBindings(ctx);   // JSClassCreate(callAsFunction) + JSObjectMake(ctx, cls, userdata)
    installJsErrorHooks(ctx); // addEventListener('error'/'unhandledrejection') forwarding error.stack
}
```
Reference implementation of the same: WebKit/WebProcess/WebPage/WebFrame.cpp:1074-1083.

Key C API (public headers, CMakeLists.txt:542-551): JSObjectRef.h:384 JSClassCreate, :412 JSObjectMake, :422 JSObjectMakeFunctionWithCallback, :490 JSObjectMakeDeferredPromise, :647 JSObjectGetPrivate; JSValueRef.h:185 JSValueIsBigInt, :293 JSValueCompareInt64, :395/:405/:416 JSBigIntCreateWith{Int64,UInt64,String}, :428 JSValueMakeFromJSONString, :439 JSValueCreateJSONString, :495/:506 JSValueTo{Int64,UInt64} (truncating). Private: JSContextRefPrivate.h:47 JSContextCreateBacktrace (stack in native callbacks).

Console/error hooks: implement WebCore::ChromeClient::addMessageToConsole(MessageSource, MessageLevel, const String&, unsigned line, unsigned column, const String& sourceID) (page/ChromeClient.h:266); never use an ephemeral session; consider patching FrameConsoleClient::messageWithTypeAndLevel (FrameConsoleClient.cpp:198-250) to forward the full Inspector::ConsoleMessage.

Inspector (dev profile), local in-process (no WebKit2): subclass WebCore::InspectorBackendClient (openLocalFrontend -> create 2nd ZeroG view loading WebInspectorUI Main.html), subclass WebCore::InspectorFrontendClientLocal (ctor at InspectorFrontendClientLocal.h:68), call pageInspectorController.connectFrontend(channel). Model: WebKitLegacy/mac/WebCoreSupport/WebInspectorClient.{h,mm}. Remote socket alternative: ENABLE_REMOTE_INSPECTOR=ON + USE_INSPECTOR_SOCKET_SERVER=ON + include(inspector/remote/Socket.cmake); RemoteInspector::singleton().setBackendCommandsPath(...InspectorBackendCommands.js); JSRemoteInspectorServerStart("127.0.0.1", port); plus a custom RemoteInspectionTarget per Page (model: WebKit/UIProcess/Inspector/WebPageDebuggable.h).

CMake snippets for Options<ZeroG>.cmake:
```cmake
add_definitions(-DBUILDING_ZEROG__)     # plus PlatformLegacy.h patch so macOS doesn't become PLATFORM(COCOA)
add_definitions(-DJS_NO_EXPORT)          # static link; also handle WTF/JSC/WebCore EXPORT_PRIVATE macros
WEBKIT_OPTION_DEFAULT_PORT_VALUE(ENABLE_REMOTE_INSPECTOR PRIVATE OFF)  # ON only in dev profile
set(bmalloc_LIBRARY_TYPE OBJECT)
set(WTF_LIBRARY_TYPE OBJECT)
set(JavaScriptCore_LIBRARY_TYPE OBJECT)  # what ENABLE_STATIC_JSC does on PlayStation (OptionsPlayStation.cmake:362-366)
```

**Facts the doc misses**

- Upstream main (checkout 31a83a39) still has both GC suspend-signal bugs Bun fixed. Source/WTF/wtf/posix/ThreadingPOSIX.cpp:138-140 dereferences targetThread on any delivery, so a stray signal (kill -USR1) segfaults. Lines 150-161 compare currentStackPointer() with m_stack, so the suspender spins forever if any library adds SA_ONSTACK (Go cgo runtime.setsigstack, Mono, JNI) while an alt stack is installed (ASan, Crashpad). Fixes: oven-sh/WebKit PR 693 (merge ddd87b080df3, 2026-09-20) and PR 817 (merge d6375c72affe, 2026-10-21), both touching only ThreadingPOSIX.cpp.
- ThreadingPOSIX.cpp:231-233 silently overrides an existing host handler for the GC signal and only logs it (WTFLogAlways). There is no chaining, so the host loses its handler without crashing.
- JSConfigureSignalForGC(int) already exists (Source/JavaScriptCore/API/JSBasePrivate.h:58, JSBase.cpp:185-193). It is a private header, unavailable on Darwin/Windows, and must be called before WTF platform threading init (it returns false afterwards). zg_runtime_config should wrap it.
- Don't pick SIGPWR as ZeroG's default: Bun's own commit comment notes Mono's GC uses SIGPWR, and Boehm GC uses it on Linux too. Compute real-time signals at runtime (SIGRTMIN+n); glibc reserves the lowest RT signals.
- WTF/wtf/threads/Signals.cpp:590 installs trap handlers with sa_flags = SA_SIGINFO and no SA_ONSTACK, so a stack overflow on a WTF thread dies silently (oven-sh PR 742, still open). Chaining (:528-541) works only for handlers installed before JSC.
- The public Ultralight WebCore has no ChromeClient/FrameLoaderClient implementations (code search finds only EmptyClients.h / EmptyFrameLoaderClient.h). The View/compositor/event logic is the proprietary 'Ultralight' module, so ZeroG has no open single-process embedding layer for non-Apple platforms to copy.
- The Ultralight fork is at WebKit 615 / Safari 16.4.1 (2023): it predates LocalFrame.h/LocalFrameLoaderClient.h (both present upstream in Source/WebCore/page/LocalFrame.h and loader/LocalFrameLoaderClient.h) and still ships the removed curl ResourceHandle code, so its code needs heavy porting to main.
- After WebKit PR 11700 the curl NetworkStorageSession (cookies), NetworkDataTask and WebSocket implementations live only in Source/WebKit/NetworkProcess/curl (NetworkStorageSessionCurl.cpp, NetworkDataTaskCurl.cpp, WebSocketTaskCurl.cpp), i.e. WebKit2. WebCore keeps only CurlRequest/CurlStream/CookieJarDB etc. (Source/WebCore/platform/Curl.cmake). ResourceHandle under USE(CURL) is ASSERT_NOT_REACHED stubs (ResourceHandle.cpp:312-330). This also answers the doc's open WebSocket question: WebCore has CurlStream/CurlStreamScheduler but no WebSocket channel implementation.
- Source/WebCore/platform/Curl.cmake links CURL::libcurl, LibPSL::LibPSL and OpenSSL::SSL unconditionally, so libpsl is mandatory, not optional.
- Skia CMake (Source/ThirdParty/skia/CMakeLists.txt:4-7) requires Freetype 2.9 and Fontconfig 2.13 on every platform except WIN32 and PLAYSTATION, macOS included. The doc's 'Skia font manager instead of Fontconfig' needs a patch to this file in patches/.
- 102 WTF files (Vector.h, HashMap.h, RefPtr.h, StringImpl, WTFString, FastMalloc.h, config.h...) are LGPL, so WTF is not BSD-only. Some WebCore files are LGPL-2.0-only (rendering/AutoTableLayout.cpp:7-10, FixedTableLayout.*, TableLayout.h), and 13 WebCore files plus WTF DateMath and JSC JSDateMath are MPL-1.1/GPL/LGPL tri-licensed. WebCore/xml/XPathGrammar.* is Bison GPL-with-exception output.
- BoringSSL at main is Apache-2.0 (NOTICE obligations; GPLv2-incompatible). libjpeg-turbo (IJG credit) and FreeType FTL both require a credit line in product documentation when statically linked; the notice generator must surface these.
- The PlayStation port can't be built by third parties: it requires Sony SDK libraries (OptionsPlayStation.cmake:98 SceCairoForWebKit, WPEBackendPlayStation, ToolKitten at :347-350). USE_SKIA defaults OFF there (WebKitFeatures.cmake:363). JSCOnly has no WebCore, so it has no curl and no Skia.
- Servo has moved well past 0.1.0: 0.7.0 on 2026-10-05, with an LTS line 0.1.x (0.1.4 on 2026-10-02). Blitz is now 'beta', not pre-alpha.
- The lists.webkit.org pipermail links used as sources returned HTTP 403 to automated fetch. Prefer bugs.webkit.org / commits.webkit.org URLs in the doc (e.g. https://bugs.webkit.org/show_bug.cgi?id=254141, https://commits.webkit.org/261865@main, https://bugs.webkit.org/show_bug.cgi?id=223069).
- gh is installed at 'C:/Program Files/GitHub CLI/gh.exe' but is not on PATH for the Bash/PowerShell tools. It is authenticated as dzonerzy.

**Facts the doc misses**

- Roadmap diagram (L681, '7 fasi, un gate per fase') is not readable as text. Inferred from the text: Phase 0 = toolchain/bootstrap, i.e. a static WTF+JSC build from the JSCOnly config on Linux and Windows plus measured build/iteration times (L526, L683 'durate ... dopo la fase 0'). Phase 1 = the critical gate: WebCore renders a page through the custom embedding layer without WebKit2, with Skia (L679), and first metrics are taken (L664-672: size, RSS, startup to first frame, CSS animation frame time, so phase 1 probably includes basic presentation). The Windows GPU prototype is probably a gate in phase 1 or 2 (L735). Phase 2 = stable C API plus JS bindings (L673 binding latency 'dopo la fase 2', L683 'API C della fase 2'). Phases 3 and 4 run in parallel with 5 and are probably network/storage/app:// (curl, BoringSSL, cookies, WebSocket, dev mode) and input/IME/clipboard/window mode/fonts/GPU backends, in an unknown order. Phase 5 = the Zig framework (it can start once the phase 2 API is stable). Phase 6 = probably release hardening: macOS, LGPL tooling (relink kit, notices, corresponding source), packaging, fuzzing/CI, first release. Unknown: the exact scope of each phase, the gate criteria, which phase includes macOS, and whether v2 items (compositor, media, accessibility) are phase 6 or after it.
- Architecture diagram (L55, 'un processo, sei livelli') is not readable either. The six levels are probably the L67-74 table (WTF, JSC, WebCore, platform layer, embedding layer, C API) with the app above. Confirm whether SDL3, Skia and ANGLE count as separate levels.
- JSC/WTF configuration is permanently frozen at first VM creation (Source/WTF/wtf/WTFConfig.cpp:190-197, Source/JavaScriptCore/runtime/VM.cpp:568), and signal handlers can only be added during init (Source/WTF/wtf/threads/Signals.cpp:74). Every JIT, signal, GC-thread-count and wasm option must be fixed in zg_runtime_create before any view exists.
- Upstream GC signal API: JSConfigureSignalForGC (Source/JavaScriptCore/API/JSBase.cpp:185-193) must be called before WTF threading init. Without it, WTF defaults to SIGUSR1 and only logs on conflict (Source/WTF/wtf/posix/ThreadingPOSIX.cpp:198-232).
- On macOS, WTF traps go through Mach exception ports (Signals.cpp:126-141, 381-387), which conflicts with Crashpad and Breakpad on macOS. The doc's L155 'riguarda Linux' is incomplete.
- Web Workers each create their own JSC VM (Source/WebCore/workers/WorkerGlobalScope.cpp:117), so 'una sola VM' (L135) holds only for page JS, and the per-runtime heap limit must account for worker VMs.
- WebCore RunLoopObserver does nothing on the generic event loop (Source/WebCore/platform/RunLoopObserver.cpp:52-66). The embedding layer must schedule rendering updates itself through ChromeClient::scheduleRenderingUpdate / triggerRenderingUpdate (Source/WebCore/page/ChromeClient.h:527-529) and supply a DisplayRefreshMonitorFactory (:484) for rAF.
- Upstream Windows uses the /MD CRT (Source/cmake/OptionsMSVC.cmake:58). The doc's /MT (L519) needs an override, and it forces the host to also use /MT when linking the static archive.
- WebKit needs C++23 (Source/cmake/OptionsCommon.cmake:1-3), GCC >= 13.1 (Source/cmake/WebKitCommon.cmake:134-137), effectively Clang >= 18 with a workaround and >= 19 cleanly (Source/cmake/WebKitCompilerFlags.cmake:243-252), clang-cl only on Windows (OptionsMSVC.cmake:1), and CMake >= 3.20. glibc 2.28 is the hard floor because of statx (Source/WTF/wtf/posix/POSIXExtras.h:130).
- In-tree Skia CMake builds only Ganesh GL (Source/ThirdParty/skia/CMakeLists.txt:423-452, 856-866), hard-requires Fontconfig and FreeType on non-Windows (:4-7, 846-852), uses DirectWrite on Windows (:815-826), and needs WebP::mux when USE_SKIA_ENCODERS is on (:9-11, 879) plus JPEG and PNG always (:972-975). Vulkan, Metal, an embedded font manager and FreeType-on-Windows all require CMake patches.
- Upstream Windows ANGLE is SHARED and includes the D3D9 backend unconditionally (Source/ThirdParty/ANGLE/PlatformWin.cmake). The doc's Windows runtime DLL list (L519) misses d3d9, d3dcompiler_47, dxguid/dwrite (if native fonts), imm32/msctf (IME), winmm, dbghelp, shlwapi and synchronization (from Source/WTF/wtf/PlatformJSCOnly.cmake:24-29).
- HarfBuzz must be built with hb-icu: Source/WebCore/platform/graphics/skia/ComplexTextControllerSkia.cpp:35 and Source/WebCore/platform/text/LocaleICU.cpp:48. The doc's 'senza ICU' (L460) will not link.
- The executable JIT pool reserves 1 GB of virtual address space on x86_64 (Source/JavaScriptCore/jit/ExecutableAllocator.cpp:101-102), and FIXED_EXECUTABLE_MEMORY_POOL_SIZE_IN_MB can shrink it. Wasm fast memory (OptionsList.h:595) reserves more in the standard profile.
- WebKit vendors mimalloc with a USE_MIMALLOC option (Source/bmalloc/mimalloc, Source/cmake/WebKitFeatures.cmake:366). That is the obvious 'hardened allocator' candidate for L146/L754.
- Large static WebCore archives are a known upstream problem: Source/cmake/OptionsPlayStation.cmake:368-373 ('libWebCore.a is too much for ranlib'), so PlayStation uses OBJECT libraries. COFF .lib archives use 32-bit offsets, which matters for the dev and debug profiles.
- A WebSocket implementation is available to reuse: Source/WebKitLegacy/WebCoreSupport/WebSocketChannel.cpp, SocketStreamHandleImpl.cpp and LegacySocketProvider.cpp, plus Source/WebCore/platform/network/curl/CurlStream.cpp. In-process storage is also available: Source/WebKitLegacy/Storage/InProcessIDBServer.cpp, StorageAreaImpl.cpp, StorageNamespaceImpl.cpp. Both are BSD and support L303 and L758.
- The scheme registry is static and process-global (Source/WebCore/platform/LegacySchemeRegistry.h:49,85), and MemoryCache is a singleton (Source/WebCore/loader/cache/MemoryCache.h:98). Per-context schemes, caches and fonts are not isolatable.
- The design has no origin allowlist API, no JS-exception-throwing API for C callbacks, no zg_promise creation API, no definition of zg_value ownership, no list of thread-safe functions, and no specification for view/window ownership (does destroying zg_window destroy the view from zg_window_view?).
- macOS: AppKit, NSTextInputClient and Metal display links need the process main thread. The runtime-creating thread must be the process main thread in window mode, which the doc does not state.
- Inconsistent checklist: L745 says the name is 'deciso' but the box is unchecked. The engine code license (L555, L745) is still undecided, yet L557 requires per-file license tracking from day one. Decide before the first commit.

<details><summary>Concrete starting material</summary>

```text
Verified facts for the ZeroG Options/Platform CMake (paths relative to C:/Users/dzonerzy/CppProjects/ZeroG/third_party/webkit):

1) Toolchain floor: CMake>=3.20; C++23 (Source/cmake/OptionsCommon.cmake:1-3); clang>=19 recommended (WebKitCompilerFlags.cmake:243); clang-cl on Windows (OptionsMSVC.cmake:1); glibc>=2.28 (statx).

2) Event loop, as JSCOnly does it (OptionsJSCOnly.cmake:109-110):
   SET_AND_EXPOSE_TO_BUILD(USE_GENERIC_EVENT_LOOP 1)
   SET_AND_EXPOSE_TO_BUILD(WTF_DEFAULT_EVENT_LOOP 0)
   On WIN32, keep the win/*.cpp WTF sources from Source/WTF/wtf/PlatformJSCOnly.cmake:5-30 and add generic/RunLoopGeneric.cpp.

3) Library types: the defaults are already STATIC (Source/cmake/WebKitCommon.cmake:241-245). Prefer OBJECT for WTF, bmalloc, JSC, PAL and WebCore, as PlayStation does (OptionsPlayStation.cmake:355-373). ENABLE_STATIC_JSC is only a port-local knob (OptionsJSCOnly.cmake:19).

4) Minimal profile flag values, all of which exist in Source/cmake/WebKitFeatures.cmake:
   -DENABLE_VIDEO=OFF -DENABLE_WEB_AUDIO=OFF -DENABLE_WEBGL=OFF -DENABLE_WEBASSEMBLY=OFF -DENABLE_XSLT=OFF -DENABLE_WEB_RTC=OFF -DENABLE_MEDIA_STREAM=OFF -DENABLE_ENCRYPTED_MEDIA=OFF -DENABLE_GAMEPAD=OFF -DENABLE_SPEECH_SYNTHESIS=OFF -DENABLE_REMOTE_INSPECTOR=OFF -DUSE_AVIF=OFF -DUSE_JPEGXL=OFF -DUSE_WOFF2=ON -DUSE_SKIA=ON -DUSE_SYSTEM_MALLOC=ON (or evaluate -DUSE_MIMALLOC=ON) -DUSE_SKIA_ENCODERS=OFF (drops the libwebp mux requirement).
   ENABLE_SERVICE_WORKER does not exist; use the runtime preference ServiceWorkersEnabled=false.
   Set CMAKE_MSVC_RUNTIME_LIBRARY=MultiThreaded explicitly if /MT is kept, overriding OptionsMSVC.cmake:58.

5) Runtime init order for zg_runtime_create (each step must come before the first VM):
   JSConfigureSignalForGC(cfg.gc_signal)            // JSBase.cpp:185, Linux only
   WTF::initializeMainThread()                      // MainThread.cpp:40
   JSC::initialize(); set JSC::Options (useJIT, numberOfGCMarkers, numberOfDFGCompilerThreads, numberOfFTLCompilerThreads, useWasmFastMemory, usePollingTraps)
   if (jit==off || allow_external_content) ExecutableAllocator::disableJIT() / Options::useJIT()=false
   WebCore::commonVM()  -> WTF::Config::finalize() freezes everything (VM.cpp:568)

6) C API hooks that exist upstream: RunLoop::cycle() for zg_runtime_update (RunLoop.h:123); RunLoop::setWakeUpCallback for zg_runtime_wakeup_handle (RunLoop.h:147-148); RunLoop::main().dispatch() for thread-safe zg_promise_resolve (RunLoop.h:111); JSObjectMakeDeferredPromise for async bindings (JSObjectRef.h:490); LocalFrameLoaderClient::dispatchDidClearWindowObjectInWorld for binding install (LocalFrameLoaderClient.h:292); ChromeClient::scheduleRenderingUpdate/triggerRenderingUpdate/displayRefreshMonitorFactory for frame and rAF scheduling (ChromeClient.h:484-529); LegacySchemeRegistry::registerURLSchemeAsSecure/registerURLSchemeAsCORSEnabled for app:// (LegacySchemeRegistry.h:49,85).

7) Proposed doc edits, in priority order:
   (a) the sync/async marker plus a fix to the L620 example;
   (b) a list of thread-safe functions, with zg_promise_resolve taking serialized data;
   (c) external content and JIT moved into zg_runtime_config;
   (d) the rAF clock defined per mode, plus a zg_view_render(view, timestamp) signature;
   (e) the dependency recount: HarfBuzz with ICU, Fontconfig patched out of Skia, SDL3 and zstd listed, WebP tied to Skia encoders;
   (f) the /MT host constraint, or a dual build;
   (g) per-OS symbol localization recipes (ELF: ld.lld -r + llvm-objcopy --localize-hidden; Mach-O: ld -r -exported_symbols_list; COFF: per-library symbol prefixes or a DLL);
   (h) no cross-boundary LTO, and the relink kit ships the prelink scripts and pinned toolchain;
   (i) dev vs debug profile split, and an inspector window exception to the 'one window in v1' rule;
   (j) an allowed_origins list in zg_context_config covering dev-server origins.
